/root/omarchy-iso/manifests/fresh-4-semantic.json:214:        "text": "# /etc/default/ufw\n#\n\n# Set to yes to apply rules to support IPv6 (no means only IPv6 on loopback\n# accepted). You will need to 'disable' and then 'enable' the firewall for\n# the changes to take affect.\nIPV6=yes\n\n# Set the default input policy to ACCEPT, DROP, or REJECT. Please note that if\n# you change this you will most likely want to adjust your rules.\nDEFAULT_INPUT_POLICY=\"DROP\"\n\n# Set the default output policy to ACCEPT, DROP, or REJECT. Please note that if\n# you change this you will most likely want to adjust your rules.\nDEFAULT_OUTPUT_POLICY=\"ACCEPT\"\n\n# Set the default forward policy to ACCEPT, DROP or REJECT.  Please note that\n# if you change this you will most likely want to adjust your rules\nDEFAULT_FORWARD_POLICY=\"DROP\"\n\n# Set the default application policy to ACCEPT, DROP, REJECT or SKIP. Please\n# note that setting this to ACCEPT may be a security risk. See 'man ufw' for\n# details\nDEFAULT_APPLICATION_POLICY=\"SKIP\"\n\n# By default, ufw only touches its own chains. Set this to 'yes' to have ufw\n# manage the built-in chains too. Warning: setting this to 'yes' will break\n# non-ufw managed firewall rules\nMANAGE_BUILTINS=no\n\n#\n# IPT backend\n#\n# only enable if using iptables backend\nIPT_SYSCTL=/etc/ufw/sysctl.conf\n\n# Extra connection tracking modules to load. IPT_MODULES should typically be\n# empty for new installations and modules added only as needed. See\n# 'CONNECTION HELPERS' from 'man ufw-framework' for details. Complete list can\n# be found in net/netfilter/Kconfig of your kernel source. Some common modules:\n# nf_conntrack_irc, nf_nat_irc: DCC (Direct Client to Client) support\n# nf_conntrack_netbios_ns: NetBIOS (samba) client support\n# nf_conntrack_pptp, nf_nat_pptp: PPTP over stateful firewall/NAT\n# nf_conntrack_ftp, nf_nat_ftp: active FTP support\n# nf_conntrack_tftp, nf_nat_tftp: TFTP support (server side)\n# nf_conntrack_sane: sane support\nIPT_MODULES=\"\"\n",
/root/omarchy-iso/manifests/fresh-4-semantic.json:255:        "text": "# vim:set ft=sh:\n# MODULES\n# The following modules are loaded before any boot hooks are\n# run.  Advanced users may wish to specify all system modules\n# in this array.  For instance:\n#     MODULES=(usbhid xhci_hcd)\nMODULES=()\n\n# BINARIES\n# This setting includes any additional binaries a given user may\n# wish into the CPIO image.  This is run last, so it may be used to\n# override the actual binaries included by a given hook\n# BINARIES are dependency parsed, so you may safely ignore libraries\nBINARIES=()\n\n# FILES\n# This setting is similar to BINARIES above, however, files are added\n# as-is and are not parsed in any way.  This is useful for config files.\nFILES=()\n\n# HOOKS\n# This is the most important setting in this file.  The HOOKS control the\n# modules and scripts added to the image, and what happens at boot time.\n# Order is important, and it is recommended that you do not change the\n# order in which HOOKS are added.  Run 'mkinitcpio -H <hook name>' for\n# help on a given hook.\n# 'base' is _required_ unless you know precisely what you are doing.\n# 'udev' is _required_ in order to automatically load modules\n# 'filesystems' is _required_ unless you specify your fs modules in MODULES\n# Examples:\n##   This setup specifies all modules in the MODULES setting above.\n##   No RAID, lvm2, or encrypted root is needed.\n#    HOOKS=(base)\n#\n##   This setup will autodetect all modules for your system and should\n##   work as a sane default\n#    HOOKS=(base udev autodetect microcode modconf block filesystems fsck)\n#\n##   This setup will generate a 'full' image which supports most systems.\n##   No autodetection is done.\n#    HOOKS=(base udev microcode modconf block filesystems fsck)\n#\n##   This setup assembles a mdadm array with an encrypted root file system.\n##   Note: See 'mkinitcpio -H mdadm_udev' for more information on RAID devices.\n#    HOOKS=(base udev microcode modconf keyboard keymap consolefont block mdadm_udev encrypt filesystems fsck)\n#\n##   This setup loads an lvm2 volume group.\n#    HOOKS=(base udev microcode modconf block lvm2 filesystems fsck)\n#\n##   This will create a systemd based initramfs which loads an encrypted root filesystem.\n#    HOOKS=(base systemd autodetect microcode modconf kms keyboard sd-vconsole block sd-encrypt filesystems fsck)\n#\n##   NOTE: If you have /usr on a separate partition, you MUST include the\n#    usr and fsck hooks.\nHOOKS=(base systemd autodetect microcode modconf kms keyboard sd-vconsole block filesystems fsck)\n\n# COMPRESSION\n# Use this to compress the initramfs image. By default, zstd compression\n# is used for Linux \u2265 5.9 and gzip compression is used for Linux < 5.9.\n# Use 'cat' to create an uncompressed image.\n#COMPRESSION=\"zstd\"\n#COMPRESSION=\"gzip\"\n#COMPRESSION=\"bzip2\"\n#COMPRESSION=\"lzma\"\n#COMPRESSION=\"xz\"\n#COMPRESSION=\"lzop\"\n#COMPRESSION=\"lz4\"\n\n# COMPRESSION_OPTIONS\n# Additional options for the compressor\n#COMPRESSION_OPTIONS=()\n\n# MODULES_DECOMPRESS\n# Decompress loadable kernel modules and their firmware during initramfs\n# creation. Switch (yes/no).\n# Enable to allow further decreasing image size when using high compression\n# (e.g. xz -9e or zstd --long --ultra -22) at the expense of increased RAM usage\n# at early boot.\n# Note that any compressed files will be placed in the uncompressed early CPIO\n# to avoid double compression.\n#MODULES_DECOMPRESS=\"no\"\n",
/root/omarchy-iso/manifests/fresh-4-semantic.json:309:      "/etc/pacman.d/hooks/90-mkinitcpio-install.hook": {
/root/omarchy-iso/manifests/fresh-4-semantic.json:315:        "text": "[Trigger]\nType = Path\nOperation = Install\nOperation = Upgrade\nOperation = Remove\nTarget = usr/lib/initcpio/*\nTarget = usr/lib/firmware/*\nTarget = usr/lib/modules/*/extramodules/\nTarget = usr/src/*/dkms.conf\nTarget = usr/lib/systemd/systemd\nTarget = usr/bin/cryptsetup\nTarget = usr/bin/lvm\n\n[Trigger]\nType = Path\nOperation = Install\nOperation = Upgrade\nTarget = usr/lib/modules/*/pkgbase\n\n[Trigger]\nType = Package\nOperation = Install\nOperation = Upgrade\nTarget = mkinitcpio\nTarget = mkinitcpio-git\n\n[Action]\nDescription = Updating linux initcpios...\nWhen = PostTransaction\nExec = /usr/share/libalpm/scripts/limine-mkinitcpio-install\nNeedsTargets\n",

/root/omarchy-iso/manifests/fresh-4-semantic.json:451:        "text": "#\n# https://wiki.documentfoundation.org/Development/Environment_variables\n#\n\n# to force a certain look'n feel\n\n#export SAL_USE_VCLPLUGIN=gen\n#export SAL_USE_VCLPLUGIN=kf6\n#export SAL_USE_VCLPLUGIN=qt6\n#export SAL_USE_VCLPLUGIN=gtk3\n#export SAL_USE_VCLPLUGIN=gtk4\n",
/root/omarchy-iso/manifests/fresh-4-semantic.json:588:        "text": "#\n# This is the configuration file for the pam_group module.\n#\n\n#\n# *** Please note that giving group membership on a session basis is\n# *** NOT inherently secure. If a user can create an executable that\n# *** is setgid a group that they are infrequently given membership\n# *** of, they can basically obtain group membership any time they\n# *** like. Example: games are allowed between the hours of 6pm and 6am\n# *** user joe logs in at 7pm writes a small C-program toplay.c that\n# *** invokes their favorite shell, compiles it and does\n# *** \"chgrp play toplay; chmod g+s toplay\". They are basically able\n# *** to play games any time... You have been warned. AGM\n#\n\n#\n# The syntax of the lines is as follows:\n#\n#       services;ttys;users;times;groups\n#\n# white space is ignored and lines maybe extended with '\\\\n' (escaped\n# newlines). From reading these comments, it is clear that\n# text following a '#' is ignored to the end of the line.\n#\n# the combination of individual users/terminals etc is a logic list\n# namely individual tokens that are optionally prefixed with '!' (logical\n# not) and separated with '&' (logical and) and '|' (logical or).\n#\n# services\n#       is a logic list of PAM service names that the rule applies to.\n#\n# ttys\n#       is a logic list of terminal names that this rule applies to.\n#\n# users\n#       is a logic list of users or a netgroup of users to whom this\n#       rule applies.\n#\n# NB. For these items the simple wildcard '*' may be used only once.\n#     With netgroups no wildcards or logic operators are allowed.\n#\n# times\n#       It is used to indicate \"when\" these groups are to be given to the\n#       user. The format here is a logic list of day/time-range\n#       entries the days are specified by a sequence of two character\n#       entries, MoTuSa for example is Monday Tuesday and Saturday. Note\n#       that repeated days are unset MoMo = no day, and MoWk = all weekdays\n#       bar Monday. The two character combinations accepted are\n#\n#               Mo Tu We Th Fr Sa Su Wk Wd Al\n#\n#       the last two being week-end days and all 7 days of the week\n#       respectively. As a final example, AlFr means all days except Friday.\n#\n#       Each day/time-range can be prefixed with a '!' to indicate \"anything\n#       but\"\n#\n#       The time-range part is two 24-hour times HHMM separated by a hyphen\n#       indicating the start and finish time (if the finish time is smaller\n#       than the start time it is deemed to apply on the following day).\n#\n# groups\n#\tThe (comma or space separated) list of groups that the user\n#\tinherits membership of. These groups are added if the previous\n#\tfields are satisfied by the user's request\n#\n# For a rule to be active, ALL of service+ttys+users must be satisfied\n# by the applying process.\n#\n\n#\n# Note, to get this to work as it is currently typed you need\n#\n# 1. to run an application as root\n# 2. add the following groups to the /etc/group file:\n#\t\tfloppy, play, sound\n#\n\n#\n# Here is a simple example: running 'xsh' on tty* (any ttyXXX device),\n# the user 'us' is given access to the floppy (through membership of\n# the floppy group)\n#\n\n#xsh;tty*&!ttyp*;us;Al0000-2400;floppy\n\n#\n# another example: running 'xsh' on tty* (any ttyXXX device),\n# the user 'sword' is given access to games (through membership of\n# the sound and play group) after work hours.\n#\n\n#xsh; tty* ;sword;!Wk0900-1800;sound, play\n#xsh; tty* ;*;Al0900-1800;floppy\n\n#\n# yet another example: any member of the group 'admin' running\n# 'xsh' on tty*, is granted access (at any time) to the group 'plugdev'\n#\n\n#xsh; tty* ;%admin;Al0000-2400;plugdev\n\n#\n# End of group.conf file\n#\n",
/root/omarchy-iso/manifests/fresh-4-semantic.json:654:        "text": "# Configuration for systemwide password quality limits\n# Defaults:\n#\n# Number of characters in the new password that must not be present in the\n# old password.\n# difok = 1\n#\n# Minimum acceptable size for the new password (plus one if\n# credits are not disabled which is the default). (See pam_cracklib manual.)\n# Cannot be set to lower value than 6.\n# minlen = 8\n#\n# The maximum credit for having digits in the new password. If less than 0\n# it is the minimum number of digits in the new password.\n# dcredit = 0\n#\n# The maximum credit for having uppercase characters in the new password.\n# If less than 0 it is the minimum number of uppercase characters in the new\n# password.\n# ucredit = 0\n#\n# The maximum credit for having lowercase characters in the new password.\n# If less than 0 it is the minimum number of lowercase characters in the new\n# password.\n# lcredit = 0\n#\n# The maximum credit for having other characters in the new password.\n# If less than 0 it is the minimum number of other characters in the new\n# password.\n# ocredit = 0\n#\n# The minimum number of required classes of characters for the new\n# password (digits, uppercase, lowercase, others).\n# minclass = 0\n#\n# The maximum number of allowed consecutive same characters in the new password.\n# The check is disabled if the value is 0.\n# maxrepeat = 0\n#\n# The maximum number of allowed consecutive characters of the same class in the\n# new password.\n# The check is disabled if the value is 0.\n# maxclassrepeat = 0\n#\n# Whether to check for the words from the passwd entry GECOS string of the user.\n# The check is enabled if the value is not 0.\n# gecoscheck = 0\n#\n# Whether to check for the words from the cracklib dictionary.\n# The check is enabled if the value is not 0.\n# dictcheck = 1\n#\n# Whether to check if it contains the user name in some form.\n# The check is enabled if the value is not 0.\n# usercheck = 1\n#\n# Length of substrings from the username to check for in the password\n# The check is enabled if the value is greater than 0 and usercheck is enabled.\n# usersubstr = 0\n#\n# Whether the check is enforced by the PAM module and possibly other\n# applications.\n# The new password is rejected if it fails the check and the value is not 0.\n# enforcing = 1\n#\n# Path to the cracklib dictionaries. Default is to use the cracklib default.\n# dictpath =\n#\n# Prompt user at most N times before returning with error. The default is 1.\n# retry = 3\n#\n# Enforces pwquality checks on the root user password.\n# Enabled if the option is present.\n# enforce_for_root\n#\n# Skip testing the password quality for users that are not present in the\n# /etc/passwd file.\n# Enabled if the option is present.\n# local_users_only\n",
/root/omarchy-iso/manifests/fresh-4-semantic.json:877:        "text": "[Telnet]\ntitle=Telnet server (insecure)\ndescription=Telnet server (insecure)\nports=23/tcp\n\n[SSH]\ntitle=SSH server\ndescription=SSH server\nports=22/tcp\n\n[VNC]\ntitle=VNC server\ndescription=VNC server\nports=5900/tcp\n",
/root/omarchy-iso/manifests/fresh-4-semantic.json:953:        "text": "#\n# rules.before\n#\n# Rules that should be run before the ufw command line added rules. Custom\n# rules should be added to one of these chains:\n#   ufw6-before-input\n#   ufw6-before-output\n#   ufw6-before-forward\n#\n\n# Don't delete these required lines, otherwise there will be errors\n*filter\n:ufw6-before-input - [0:0]\n:ufw6-before-output - [0:0]\n:ufw6-before-forward - [0:0]\n# End required lines\n\n\n# allow all on loopback\n-A ufw6-before-input -i lo -j ACCEPT\n-A ufw6-before-output -o lo -j ACCEPT\n\n# drop packets with RH0 headers\n-A ufw6-before-input -m rt --rt-type 0 -j DROP\n-A ufw6-before-forward -m rt --rt-type 0 -j DROP\n-A ufw6-before-output -m rt --rt-type 0 -j DROP\n\n# quickly process packets for which we already have a connection\n-A ufw6-before-input -m conntrack --ctstate RELATED,ESTABLISHED -j ACCEPT\n-A ufw6-before-output -m conntrack --ctstate RELATED,ESTABLISHED -j ACCEPT\n-A ufw6-before-forward -m conntrack --ctstate RELATED,ESTABLISHED -j ACCEPT\n\n# multicast ping replies are part of the ok icmp codes for INPUT (rfc4890,\n# 4.4.1 and 4.4.2), but don't have an associated connection and are otherwise\n# be marked INVALID, so allow here instead.\n-A ufw6-before-input -p icmpv6 --icmpv6-type echo-reply -j ACCEPT\n\n# drop INVALID packets (logs these in loglevel medium and higher)\n-A ufw6-before-input -m conntrack --ctstate INVALID -j ufw6-logging-deny\n-A ufw6-before-input -m conntrack --ctstate INVALID -j DROP\n\n# ok icmp codes for INPUT (rfc4890, 4.4.1 and 4.4.2)\n-A ufw6-before-input -p icmpv6 --icmpv6-type destination-unreachable -j ACCEPT\n-A ufw6-before-input -p icmpv6 --icmpv6-type packet-too-big -j ACCEPT\n# codes 0 and 1\n-A ufw6-before-input -p icmpv6 --icmpv6-type time-exceeded -j ACCEPT\n# codes 0-2 (echo-reply needs to be before INVALID, see above)\n-A ufw6-before-input -p icmpv6 --icmpv6-type parameter-problem -j ACCEPT\n-A ufw6-before-input -p icmpv6 --icmpv6-type echo-request -j ACCEPT\n-A ufw6-before-input -p icmpv6 --icmpv6-type router-solicitation -m hl --hl-eq 255 -j ACCEPT\n-A ufw6-before-input -p icmpv6 --icmpv6-type router-advertisement -m hl --hl-eq 255 -j ACCEPT\n-A ufw6-before-input -p icmpv6 --icmpv6-type neighbor-solicitation -m hl --hl-eq 255 -j ACCEPT\n-A ufw6-before-input -p icmpv6 --icmpv6-type neighbor-advertisement -m hl --hl-eq 255 -j ACCEPT\n# IND solicitation\n-A ufw6-before-input -p icmpv6 --icmpv6-type 141 -m hl --hl-eq 255 -j ACCEPT\n# IND advertisement\n-A ufw6-before-input -p icmpv6 --icmpv6-type 142 -m hl --hl-eq 255 -j ACCEPT\n# MLD query\n-A ufw6-before-input -p icmpv6 --icmpv6-type 130 -s fe80::/10 -j ACCEPT\n# MLD report\n-A ufw6-before-input -p icmpv6 --icmpv6-type 131 -s fe80::/10 -j ACCEPT\n# MLD done\n-A ufw6-before-input -p icmpv6 --icmpv6-type 132 -s fe80::/10 -j ACCEPT\n# MLD report v2\n-A ufw6-before-input -p icmpv6 --icmpv6-type 143 -s fe80::/10 -j ACCEPT\n# SEND certificate path solicitation\n-A ufw6-before-input -p icmpv6 --icmpv6-type 148 -m hl --hl-eq 255 -j ACCEPT\n# SEND certificate path advertisement\n-A ufw6-before-input -p icmpv6 --icmpv6-type 149 -m hl --hl-eq 255 -j ACCEPT\n# MR advertisement\n-A ufw6-before-input -p icmpv6 --icmpv6-type 151 -s fe80::/10 -m hl --hl-eq 1 -j ACCEPT\n# MR solicitation\n-A ufw6-before-input -p icmpv6 --icmpv6-type 152 -s fe80::/10 -m hl --hl-eq 1 -j ACCEPT\n# MR termination\n-A ufw6-before-input -p icmpv6 --icmpv6-type 153 -s fe80::/10 -m hl --hl-eq 1 -j ACCEPT\n\n# ok icmp codes for OUTPUT (rfc4890, 4.4.1 and 4.4.2)\n-A ufw6-before-output -p icmpv6 --icmpv6-type destination-unreachable -j ACCEPT\n-A ufw6-before-output -p icmpv6 --icmpv6-type packet-too-big -j ACCEPT\n# codes 0 and 1\n-A ufw6-before-output -p icmpv6 --icmpv6-type time-exceeded -j ACCEPT\n# codes 0-2\n-A ufw6-before-output -p icmpv6 --icmpv6-type parameter-problem -j ACCEPT\n-A ufw6-before-output -p icmpv6 --icmpv6-type echo-request -j ACCEPT\n-A ufw6-before-output -p icmpv6 --icmpv6-type echo-reply -j ACCEPT\n-A ufw6-before-output -p icmpv6 --icmpv6-type router-solicitation -m hl --hl-eq 255 -j ACCEPT\n-A ufw6-before-output -p icmpv6 --icmpv6-type neighbor-advertisement -m hl --hl-eq 255 -j ACCEPT\n-A ufw6-before-output -p icmpv6 --icmpv6-type neighbor-solicitation -m hl --hl-eq 255 -j ACCEPT\n-A ufw6-before-output -p icmpv6 --icmpv6-type router-advertisement -m hl --hl-eq 255 -j ACCEPT\n# IND solicitation\n-A ufw6-before-output -p icmpv6 --icmpv6-type 141 -m hl --hl-eq 255 -j ACCEPT\n# IND advertisement\n-A ufw6-before-output -p icmpv6 --icmpv6-type 142 -m hl --hl-eq 255 -j ACCEPT\n# MLD query\n-A ufw6-before-output -p icmpv6 --icmpv6-type 130 -s fe80::/10 -j ACCEPT\n# MLD report\n-A ufw6-before-output -p icmpv6 --icmpv6-type 131 -s fe80::/10 -j ACCEPT\n# MLD done\n-A ufw6-before-output -p icmpv6 --icmpv6-type 132 -s fe80::/10 -j ACCEPT\n# MLD report v2\n-A ufw6-before-output -p icmpv6 --icmpv6-type 143 -s fe80::/10 -j ACCEPT\n# SEND certificate path solicitation\n-A ufw6-before-output -p icmpv6 --icmpv6-type 148 -m hl --hl-eq 255 -j ACCEPT\n# SEND certificate path advertisement\n-A ufw6-before-output -p icmpv6 --icmpv6-type 149 -m hl --hl-eq 255 -j ACCEPT\n# MR advertisement\n-A ufw6-before-output -p icmpv6 --icmpv6-type 151 -s fe80::/10 -m hl --hl-eq 1 -j ACCEPT\n# MR solicitation\n-A ufw6-before-output -p icmpv6 --icmpv6-type 152 -s fe80::/10 -m hl --hl-eq 1 -j ACCEPT\n# MR termination\n-A ufw6-before-output -p icmpv6 --icmpv6-type 153 -s fe80::/10 -m hl --hl-eq 1 -j ACCEPT\n\n# ok icmp codes for FORWARD (rfc4890, 4.3.1)\n-A ufw6-before-forward -p icmpv6 --icmpv6-type destination-unreachable -j ACCEPT\n-A ufw6-before-forward -p icmpv6 --icmpv6-type packet-too-big -j ACCEPT\n# codes 0 and 1\n-A ufw6-before-forward -p icmpv6 --icmpv6-type time-exceeded -j ACCEPT\n# codes 0-2\n-A ufw6-before-forward -p icmpv6 --icmpv6-type parameter-problem -j ACCEPT\n-A ufw6-before-forward -p icmpv6 --icmpv6-type echo-request -j ACCEPT\n-A ufw6-before-forward -p icmpv6 --icmpv6-type echo-reply -j ACCEPT\n# ok icmp codes for FORWARD (rfc4890, 4.3.2)\n# Home Agent Address Discovery Reques\n-A ufw6-before-input -p icmpv6 --icmpv6-type 144 -j ACCEPT\n# Home Agent Address Discovery Reply\n-A ufw6-before-input -p icmpv6 --icmpv6-type 145 -j ACCEPT\n# Mobile Prefix Solicitation\n-A ufw6-before-input -p icmpv6 --icmpv6-type 146 -j ACCEPT\n# Mobile Prefix Advertisement\n-A ufw6-before-input -p icmpv6 --icmpv6-type 147 -j ACCEPT\n\n# allow dhcp client to work\n-A ufw6-before-input -p udp -s fe80::/10 --sport 547 -d fe80::/10 --dport 546 -j ACCEPT\n\n# allow MULTICAST mDNS for service discovery\n-A ufw6-before-input -p udp -d ff02::fb --dport 5353 -j ACCEPT\n\n# allow MULTICAST UPnP for service discovery\n-A ufw6-before-input -p udp -d ff02::f --dport 1900 -j ACCEPT\n\n# don't delete the 'COMMIT' line or these rules won't be processed\nCOMMIT\n",
/root/omarchy-iso/manifests/fresh-4-semantic.json:964:        "text": "#\n# Configuration file for setting network variables. Please note these settings\n# override /etc/sysctl.conf. If you prefer to use /etc/sysctl.conf, please\n# adjust IPT_SYSCTL in /etc/default/ufw.\n#\n\n# Uncomment this to allow this host to route packets between interfaces\n#net/ipv4/ip_forward=1\n#net/ipv6/conf/default/forwarding=1\n#net/ipv6/conf/all/forwarding=1\n\n# Turn on Source Address Verification in all interfaces to prevent some\n# spoofing attacks\nnet/ipv4/conf/default/rp_filter=1\nnet/ipv4/conf/all/rp_filter=1\n\n# Do not accept IP source route packets (we are not a router)\nnet/ipv4/conf/default/accept_source_route=0\nnet/ipv4/conf/all/accept_source_route=0\nnet/ipv6/conf/default/accept_source_route=0\nnet/ipv6/conf/all/accept_source_route=0\n\n# Disable ICMP redirects. ICMP redirects are rarely used but can be used in\n# MITM (man-in-the-middle) attacks. Disabling ICMP may disrupt legitimate\n# traffic to those sites.\nnet/ipv4/conf/default/accept_redirects=0\nnet/ipv4/conf/all/accept_redirects=0\nnet/ipv6/conf/default/accept_redirects=0\nnet/ipv6/conf/all/accept_redirects=0\n\n# Ignore bogus ICMP errors\nnet/ipv4/icmp_echo_ignore_broadcasts=1\nnet/ipv4/icmp_ignore_bogus_error_responses=1\nnet/ipv4/icmp_echo_ignore_all=0\n\n# Don't log Martian Packets (impossible packets)\nnet/ipv4/conf/default/log_martians=0\nnet/ipv4/conf/all/log_martians=0\n\n# Change to '0' to disable TCP/IP SYN cookies. A value of '1' disables TCP\n# Window Scaling (http://lkml.org/lkml/2008/2/5/167).\n#net/ipv4/tcp_syncookies=1\n\n#net/ipv4/tcp_fin_timeout=30\n#net/ipv4/tcp_keepalive_intvl=1800\n\n# normally allowing tcp_sack is ok, but if going through OpenBSD 3.8 RELEASE or\n# earlier pf firewall, should set this to 0\n#net/ipv4/tcp_sack=1\n\n# Uncomment this to turn off ipv6 autoconfiguration\n#net/ipv6/conf/default/autoconf=0\n#net/ipv6/conf/all/autoconf=0\n\n# Uncomment this to enable ipv6 privacy addressing\n#net/ipv6/conf/default/use_tempaddr=2\n#net/ipv6/conf/all/use_tempaddr=2\n",
/root/omarchy-iso/manifests/fresh-4-semantic.json:1256:      "/etc/systemd/system/multi-user.target.wants/sshd.service": {
/root/omarchy-iso/manifests/fresh-4-semantic.json:1260:        "target": "/usr/lib/systemd/system/sshd.service",
/root/omarchy-iso/manifests/fresh-4-semantic.json:1503:      "~/.local/share/applications/Disk Usage.desktop": {
/root/omarchy-iso/manifests/fresh-4-semantic.json:1509:        "text": "[Desktop Entry]\nVersion=1.0\nName=Disk Usage\nExec=xdg-terminal-exec --app-id=TUI.float -e bash -c \"dust -r; read -n 1 -s\"\nTerminal=false\nType=Application\nIcon=disk-usage\nStartupNotify=true\n",
/root/omarchy-iso/manifests/fresh-4-semantic.json:1758:        "text": "#? Config file for btop v.1.4.6\n\n#* Name of a btop++/bpytop/bashtop formatted \".theme\" file, \"Default\" and \"TTY\" for builtin themes.\n#* Themes should be placed in \"../share/btop/themes\" relative to binary or \"$HOME/.config/btop/themes\"\ncolor_theme = \"current\"\n\n#* If the theme set background should be shown, set to False if you want terminal background transparency.\ntheme_background = true\n\n#* Sets if 24-bit truecolor should be used, will convert 24-bit colors to 256 color (6x6x6 color cube) if false.\ntruecolor = true\n\n#* Set to true to force tty mode regardless if a real tty has been detected or not.\n#* Will force 16-color mode and TTY theme, set all graph symbols to \"tty\" and swap out other non tty friendly symbols.\nforce_tty = false\n\n#* Define presets for the layout of the boxes. Preset 0 is always all boxes shown with default settings. Max 9 presets.\n#* Format: \"box_name:P:G,box_name:P:G\" P=(0 or 1) for alternate positions, G=graph symbol to use for box.\n#* Use whitespace \" \" as separator between different presets.\n#* Example: \"cpu:0:default,mem:0:tty,proc:1:default cpu:0:braille,proc:0:tty\"\npresets = \"cpu:1:default,proc:0:default cpu:0:default,mem:0:default,net:0:default cpu:0:block,net:0:tty\"\n\n#* Set to True to enable \"h,j,k,l,g,G\" keys for directional control in lists.\n#* Conflicting keys for h:\"help\" and k:\"kill\" is accessible while holding shift.\nvim_keys = true\n\n#* Rounded corners on boxes, is ignored if TTY mode is ON.\nrounded_corners = true\n\n#* Use terminal synchronized output sequences to reduce flickering on supported terminals.\nterminal_sync = true\n\n#* Default symbols to use for graph creation, \"braille\", \"block\" or \"tty\".\n#* \"braille\" offers the highest resolution but might not be included in all fonts.\n#* \"block\" has half the resolution of braille but uses more common characters.\n#* \"tty\" uses only 3 different symbols but will work with most fonts and should work in a real TTY.\n#* Note that \"tty\" only has half the horizontal resolution of the other two, so will show a shorter historical view.\ngraph_symbol = \"braille\"\n\n# Graph symbol to use for graphs in cpu box, \"default\", \"braille\", \"block\" or \"tty\".\ngraph_symbol_cpu = \"default\"\n\n# Graph symbol to use for graphs in gpu box, \"default\", \"braille\", \"block\" or \"tty\".\ngraph_symbol_gpu = \"default\"\n\n# Graph symbol to use for graphs in cpu box, \"default\", \"braille\", \"block\" or \"tty\".\ngraph_symbol_mem = \"default\"\n\n# Graph symbol to use for graphs in cpu box, \"default\", \"braille\", \"block\" or \"tty\".\ngraph_symbol_net = \"default\"\n\n# Graph symbol to use for graphs in cpu box, \"default\", \"braille\", \"block\" or \"tty\".\ngraph_symbol_proc = \"default\"\n\n#* Manually set which boxes to show. Available values are \"cpu mem net proc\" and \"gpu0\" through \"gpu5\", separate values with whitespace.\nshown_boxes = \"cpu mem net proc\"\n\n#* Update time in milliseconds, recommended 2000 ms or above for better sample times for graphs.\nupdate_ms = 2000\n\n#* Processes sorting, \"pid\" \"program\" \"arguments\" \"threads\" \"user\" \"memory\" \"cpu lazy\" \"cpu direct\",\n#* \"cpu lazy\" sorts top process over time (easier to follow), \"cpu direct\" updates top process directly.\nproc_sorting = \"cpu lazy\"\n\n#* Reverse sorting order, True or False.\nproc_reversed = false\n\n#* Show processes as a tree.\nproc_tree = false\n\n#* Use the cpu graph colors in the process list.\nproc_colors = true\n\n#* Use a darkening gradient in the process list.\nproc_gradient = true\n\n#* If process cpu usage should be of the core it's running on or usage of the total available cpu power.\nproc_per_core = false\n\n#* Show process memory as bytes instead of percent.\nproc_mem_bytes = true\n\n#* Show cpu graph for each process.\nproc_cpu_graphs = true\n\n#* Use /proc/[pid]/smaps for memory information in the process info box (very slow but more accurate)\nproc_info_smaps = false\n\n#* Show proc box on left side of screen instead of right.\nproc_left = false\n\n#* (Linux) Filter processes tied to the Linux kernel(similar behavior to htop).\nproc_filter_kernel = false\n\n#* In tree-view, always accumulate child process resources in the parent process.\nproc_aggregate = false\n\n#* Should cpu and memory usage display be preserved for dead processes when paused.\nkeep_dead_proc_usage = false\n\n#* Sets the CPU stat shown in upper half of the CPU graph, \"total\" is always available.\n#* Select from a list of detected attributes from the options menu.\ncpu_graph_upper = \"Auto\"\n\n#* Sets the CPU stat shown in lower half of the CPU graph, \"total\" is always available.\n#* Select from a list of detected attributes from the options menu.\ncpu_graph_lower = \"Auto\"\n\n#* If gpu info should be shown in the cpu box. Available values = \"Auto\", \"On\" and \"Off\".\nshow_gpu_info = \"Auto\"\n\n#* Toggles if the lower CPU graph should be inverted.\ncpu_invert_lower = true\n\n#* Set to True to completely disable the lower CPU graph.\ncpu_single_graph = false\n\n#* Show cpu box at bottom of screen instead of top.\ncpu_bottom = false\n\n#* Shows the system uptime in the CPU box.\nshow_uptime = true\n\n#* Shows the CPU package current power consumption in watts. Requires running `make setcap` or `make setuid` or running with sudo.\nshow_cpu_watts = true\n\n#* Show cpu temperature.\ncheck_temp = true\n\n#* Which sensor to use for cpu temperature, use options menu to select from list of available sensors.\ncpu_sensor = \"Auto\"\n\n#* Show temperatures for cpu cores also if check_temp is True and sensors has been found.\nshow_coretemp = true\n\n#* Set a custom mapping between core and coretemp, can be needed on certain cpus to get correct temperature for correct core.\n#* Use lm-sensors or similar to see which cores are reporting temperatures on your machine.\n#* Format \"x:y\" x=core with wrong temp, y=core with correct temp, use space as separator between multiple entries.\n#* Example: \"4:0 5:1 6:3\"\ncpu_core_map = \"\"\n\n#* Which temperature scale to use, available values: \"celsius\", \"fahrenheit\", \"kelvin\" and \"rankine\".\ntemp_scale = \"celsius\"\n\n#* Use base 10 for bits/bytes sizes, KB = 1000 instead of KiB = 1024.\nbase_10_sizes = false\n\n#* Show CPU frequency.\nshow_cpu_freq = true\n\n#* How to calculate CPU frequency, available values: \"first\", \"range\", \"lowest\", \"highest\" and \"average\".\nfreq_mode = \"first\"\n\n#* Draw a clock at top of screen, formatting according to strftime, empty string to disable.\n#* Special formatting: /host = hostname | /user = username | /uptime = system uptime\nclock_format = \"%X\"\n\n#* Update main ui in background when menus are showing, set this to false if the menus is flickering too much for comfort.\nbackground_update = true\n\n#* Custom cpu model name, empty string to disable.\ncustom_cpu_name = \"\"\n\n#* Optional filter for shown disks, should be full path of a mountpoint, separate multiple values with whitespace \" \".\n#* Only disks matching the filter will be shown. Prepend exclude= to only show disks not matching the filter. Examples: disk_filter=\"/boot /home/user\", disks_filter=\"exclude=/boot /home/user\"\ndisks_filter = \"\"\n\n#* Show graphs instead of meters for memory values.\nmem_graphs = true\n\n#* Show mem box below net box instead of above.\nmem_below_net = false\n\n#* Count ZFS ARC in cached and available memory.\nzfs_arc_cached = true\n\n#* If swap memory should be shown in memory box.\nshow_swap = true\n\n#* Show swap as a disk, ignores show_swap value above, inserts itself after first disk.\nswap_disk = true\n\n#* If mem box should be split to also show disks info.\nshow_disks = true\n\n#* Filter out non physical disks. Set this to False to include network disks, RAM disks and similar.\nonly_physical = true\n\n#* Read disks list from /etc/fstab. This also disables only_physical.\nuse_fstab = true\n\n#* Setting this to True will hide all datasets, and only show ZFS pools. (IO stats will be calculated per-pool)\nzfs_hide_datasets = false\n\n#* Set to true to show available disk space for privileged users.\ndisk_free_priv = false\n\n#* Toggles if io activity % (disk busy time) should be shown in regular disk usage view.\nshow_io_stat = true\n\n#* Toggles io mode for disks, showing big graphs for disk read/write speeds.\nio_mode = false\n\n#* Set to True to show combined read/write io graphs in io mode.\nio_graph_combined = false\n\n#* Set the top speed for the io graphs in MiB/s (100 by default), use format \"mountpoint:speed\" separate disks with whitespace \" \".\n#* Example: \"/mnt/media:100 /:20 /boot:1\".\nio_graph_speeds = \"\"\n\n#* Set fixed values for network graphs in Mebibits. Is only used if net_auto is also set to False.\nnet_download = 100\n\nnet_upload = 100\n\n#* Use network graphs auto rescaling mode, ignores any values set above and rescales down to 10 Kibibytes at the lowest.\nnet_auto = true\n\n#* Sync the auto scaling for download and upload to whichever currently has the highest scale.\nnet_sync = true\n\n#* Starts with the Network Interface specified here.\nnet_iface = \"\"\n\n#* \"True\" shows bitrates in base 10 (Kbps, Mbps). \"False\" shows bitrates in binary sizes (Kibps, Mibps, etc.). \"Auto\" uses base_10_sizes.\nbase_10_bitrate = \"Auto\"\n\n#* Show battery stats in top right if battery is present.\nshow_battery = true\n\n#* Which battery to use if multiple are present. \"Auto\" for auto detection.\nselected_battery = \"Auto\"\n\n#* Show power stats of battery next to charge indicator.\nshow_battery_watts = true\n\n#* Set loglevel for \"~/.local/state/btop.log\" levels are: \"ERROR\" \"WARNING\" \"INFO\" \"DEBUG\".\n#* The level set includes all lower levels, i.e. \"DEBUG\" will show all logging info.\nlog_level = \"WARNING\"\n\n#* Automatically save current settings to config file on exit.\nsave_config_on_exit = true\n\n#* Measure PCIe throughput on NVIDIA cards, may impact performance on certain cards.\nnvml_measure_pcie_speeds = true\n\n#* Measure PCIe throughput on AMD cards, may impact performance on certain cards.\nrsmi_measure_pcie_speeds = true\n\n#* Horizontally mirror the GPU graph.\ngpu_mirror_graph = true\n\n#* Set which GPU vendors to show. Available values are \"nvidia amd intel\"\nshown_gpus = \"nvidia amd intel\"\n\n#* Custom gpu0 model name, empty string to disable.\ncustom_gpu_name0 = \"\"\n\n#* Custom gpu1 model name, empty string to disable.\ncustom_gpu_name1 = \"\"\n\n#* Custom gpu2 model name, empty string to disable.\ncustom_gpu_name2 = \"\"\n\n#* Custom gpu3 model name, empty string to disable.\ncustom_gpu_name3 = \"\"\n\n#* Custom gpu4 model name, empty string to disable.\ncustom_gpu_name4 = \"\"\n\n#* Custom gpu5 model name, empty string to disable.\ncustom_gpu_name5 = \"\"\n",
/root/omarchy-iso/manifests/fresh-4-semantic.json:1794:        "text": "{\n  \"$schema\": \"https://github.com/fastfetch-cli/fastfetch/raw/dev/doc/json_schema.json\",\n  \"logo\": {\n    \"type\": \"file\",\n    \"source\": \"~/.config/omarchy/branding/about.txt\",\n    \"color\": { \"1\": \"green\" },\n    \"padding\": {\n      \"top\": 2,\n      \"right\": 6,\n      \"left\": 2\n    }\n  },\n  \"modules\": [\n    \"break\",\n    {\n      \"type\": \"custom\",\n      \"format\": \"\\u001b[90m\u250c\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500Hardware\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2510\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"type\": \"host\",\n      \"key\": \"\uf109 PC\",\n      \"keyColor\": \"green\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"type\": \"cpu\",\n      \"key\": \"\u2502 \u251c\uf4bc\",\n      \"showPeCoreCount\": true,\n      \"keyColor\": \"green\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"type\": \"gpu\",\n      \"key\": \"\u2502 \u251c\ue266\",\n      \"detectionMethod\": \"pci\",\n      \"keyColor\": \"green\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"type\": \"display\",\n      \"key\": \"\u2502 \u251c\udb84\udd04\",\n      \"keyColor\": \"green\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"type\": \"disk\",\n      \"key\": \"\u2502 \u251c\udb80\udeca\",\n      \"keyColor\": \"green\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"type\": \"memory\",\n      \"key\": \"\u2502 \u251c\uefc5\",\n      \"keyColor\": \"green\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"type\": \"swap\",\n      \"key\": \"\u2514 \u2514\udb81\udce1 \",\n      \"keyColor\": \"green\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"type\": \"custom\",\n      \"format\": \"\\u001b[90m\u2514\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2518\"\n    },\n    \"break\",\n    {\n      \"type\": \"custom\",\n      \"format\": \"\\u001b[90m\u250c\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500Software\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2510\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"type\": \"command\",\n      \"key\": \"\\ue900 OS\",\n      \"keyColor\": \"blue\",\n      \"text\": \"version=$(omarchy-version); echo \\\"Omarchy $version\\\"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"type\": \"command\",\n      \"key\": \"\u2502 \u251c\udb81\udd2b\",\n      \"keyColor\": \"blue\",\n      \"text\": \"channel=$(omarchy-version-channel); echo \\\"$channel\\\"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"type\": \"kernel\",\n      \"key\": \"\u2502 \u251c\uf013\",\n      \"keyColor\": \"blue\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"type\": \"wm\",\n      \"key\": \"\u2502 \u251c\uf488\",\n      \"keyColor\": \"blue\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"type\": \"de\",\n      \"key\": \"\uf488 DE\",\n      \"keyColor\": \"blue\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"type\": \"terminal\",\n      \"key\": \"\u2502 \u251c\uf489\",\n      \"keyColor\": \"blue\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"type\": \"packages\",\n      \"key\": \"\u2502 \u251c\udb80\udfd6\",\n      \"keyColor\": \"blue\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"type\": \"wmtheme\",\n      \"key\": \"\u2502 \u251c\udb80\ude7c\",\n      \"keyColor\": \"blue\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"type\": \"command\",\n      \"key\": \"\u2502 \u251c\udb83\ude0c\",\n      \"keyColor\": \"blue\",\n      \"text\": \"theme=$(omarchy-theme-current); echo -e \\\"$theme \\\\e[38m\u25cf\\\\e[37m\u25cf\\\\e[36m\u25cf\\\\e[35m\u25cf\\\\e[34m\u25cf\\\\e[33m\u25cf\\\\e[32m\u25cf\\\\e[31m\u25cf\\\"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"type\": \"terminalfont\",\n      \"key\": \"\u2514 \u2514\uf031\",\n      \"keyColor\": \"blue\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"type\": \"custom\",\n      \"format\": \"\\u001b[90m\u2514\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2518\"\n    },\n    \"break\",\n    {\n      \"type\": \"custom\",\n      \"format\": \"\\u001b[90m\u250c\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500Age / Uptime / Update\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2510\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"type\": \"command\",\n      \"key\": \"\udb86\udd9f OS Age\",\n      \"keyColor\": \"magenta\",\n      \"text\": \"echo $(( ($(date +%s) - $(stat -c %W /)) / 86400 )) days\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"type\": \"uptime\",\n      \"key\": \"\udb86\uded0 Uptime\",\n      \"keyColor\": \"magenta\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"type\": \"command\",\n      \"key\": \"\ueb29 Update\",\n      \"keyColor\": \"magenta\",\n      \"text\": \"updated=$(omarchy-version-pkgs); echo \\\"$updated\\\"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"type\": \"custom\",\n      \"format\": \"\\u001b[90m\u2514\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2518\"\n    },\n    \"break\"\n  ]\n}\n",
/root/omarchy-iso/manifests/fresh-4-semantic.json:1813:        "text": "[main]\ninclude=~/.config/omarchy/current/theme/foot.ini\nterm=xterm-256color\nfont=JetBrainsMono Nerd Font:size=9\npad=14x14\ninitial-window-mode=windowed\nworkers=0\n\n[scrollback]\nlines=10000\n\n[cursor]\nstyle=block\nblink=no\n\n[key-bindings]\nclipboard-copy=Control+Insert Control+Shift+c XF86Copy\nprimary-paste=none\nclipboard-paste=Shift+Insert Control+Shift+v XF86Paste\n",
/root/omarchy-iso/manifests/fresh-4-semantic.json:1832:        "text": "# Dynamic theme colors\nconfig-file = ?\"~/.config/omarchy/current/theme/ghostty.conf\"\n\n# Font\nfont-family = \"JetBrainsMono Nerd Font\"\nfont-style = Regular\nfont-size = 9\n\n# Window\nwindow-theme = ghostty\nwindow-padding-x = 14\nwindow-padding-y = 14\nconfirm-close-surface=false\nresize-overlay = never\ngtk-toolbar-style = flat\n\n# Cursor styling\ncursor-style = \"block\"\ncursor-style-blink = false\n\n# Cursor styling + SSH session terminfo\n# (all shell integration options must be passed together)\nshell-integration-features = no-cursor,ssh-env\n\n# Keyboard bindings\nkeybind = shift+insert=paste_from_clipboard\nkeybind = control+insert=copy_to_clipboard\n# Legacy encoding sends Alt+Shift+Enter the same as Alt+Enter; send CSI-u so tmux can match M-S-Enter.\nkeybind = alt+shift+enter=csi:13;4u\nkeybind = super+control+shift+alt+arrow_down=resize_split:down,100\nkeybind = super+control+shift+alt+arrow_up=resize_split:up,100\nkeybind = super+control+shift+alt+arrow_left=resize_split:left,100\nkeybind = super+control+shift+alt+arrow_right=resize_split:right,100\n\n# Slowdown mouse scrolling\nmouse-scroll-multiplier = 0.95\n\n# Fix general slowness on hyprland (https://github.com/ghostty-org/ghostty/discussions/3224)\nasync-backend = epoll\n",
/root/omarchy-iso/manifests/fresh-4-semantic.json:1892:        "text": "-- Application bindings.\no.bind(\"SUPER + RETURN\", \"Terminal\", { omarchy = \"terminal\" })\no.bind(\"SUPER + ALT + RETURN\", \"Tmux\", { omarchy = \"terminal-tmux\" })\no.bind(\"SUPER + SHIFT + RETURN\", \"Browser\", { omarchy = \"browser\" })\no.bind(\"SUPER + SHIFT + F\", \"File manager\", { omarchy = \"nautilus\" })\no.bind(\"SUPER + ALT + SHIFT + F\", \"File manager (cwd)\", { omarchy = \"nautilus-cwd\" })\no.bind(\"SUPER + SHIFT + B\", \"Browser\", { omarchy = \"browser\" })\no.bind(\"SUPER + SHIFT + ALT + B\", \"Browser (private)\", { omarchy = \"browser --private\" })\no.bind(\"SUPER + SHIFT + M\", \"Music\", { omarchy = \"spotify\" })\no.bind(\"SUPER + SHIFT + ALT + M\", \"Music TUI\", { tui = \"cliamp\", focus = true })\no.bind(\"SUPER + SHIFT + N\", \"Editor\", { omarchy = \"editor\" })\no.bind(\"SUPER + SHIFT + D\", \"Docker\", { tui = \"lazydocker\" })\no.bind(\"SUPER + SHIFT + G\", \"Signal\", { omarchy = \"signal\" })\no.bind(\"SUPER + SHIFT + O\", \"Obsidian\", { launch = \"obsidian\", focus = \"^obsidian$\" })\no.bind(\"SUPER + SHIFT + W\", \"Typora\", { launch = \"typora --enable-wayland-ime\" })\no.bind(\"SUPER + SHIFT + SLASH\", \"Passwords\", { launch = \"1password\" })\n\n-- Web app bindings.\no.bind(\"SUPER + SHIFT + A\", \"ChatGPT\", { webapp = \"https://chatgpt.com\" })\no.bind(\"SUPER + SHIFT + ALT + A\", \"Grok\", { webapp = \"https://grok.com\" })\no.bind(\"SUPER + SHIFT + C\", \"Calendar\", { webapp = \"https://app.hey.com/calendar/weeks/\" })\no.bind(\"SUPER + SHIFT + E\", \"Email\", { webapp = \"https://app.hey.com\" })\no.bind(\"SUPER + SHIFT + ALT + E\", \"New email\", { webapp = \"https://app.hey.com/messages/new?display=standalone&new_window=true\" })\no.bind(\"SUPER + SHIFT + Y\", \"YouTube\", { webapp = \"https://youtube.com/\" })\no.bind(\"SUPER + SHIFT + ALT + G\", \"WhatsApp\", { webapp = \"https://web.whatsapp.com/\", focus = true })\no.bind(\n  \"SUPER + SHIFT + CTRL + G\",\n  \"Google Messages\",\n  { webapp = \"https://messages.google.com/web/conversations\", focus = true }\n)\no.bind(\"SUPER + SHIFT + P\", \"Google Photos\", { webapp = \"https://photos.google.com/\", focus = true })\no.bind(\"SUPER + SHIFT + S\", \"Google Maps\", { webapp = \"https://maps.google.com/\", focus = true })\no.bind(\"SUPER + SHIFT + X\", \"X\", { webapp = \"https://x.com/\" })\no.bind(\"SUPER + SHIFT + ALT + X\", \"X Post\", { webapp = \"https://x.com/compose/post\" })\n\n-- Add extra bindings below.\n-- o.bind(\"SUPER + SHIFT + R\", \"SSH\", \"alacritty -e ssh your-server\")\n\n-- Overwrite existing bindings with hl.unbind() first if needed.\n-- hl.unbind(\"SUPER + SPACE\")\n-- o.bind(\"SUPER + SPACE\", \"Omarchy menu\", \"omarchy-menu\")\n\n-- Logitech MX Keys examples:\n-- o.bind(\"SUPER + SHIFT + S\", nil, \"omarchy-capture-screenshot\")\n-- o.bind(\"SUPER + H\", nil, \"voxtype record toggle\")\n-- o.bind(\"SUPER + PERIOD\", nil, { omarchy = \"walker -m symbols\" })\n",
/root/omarchy-iso/manifests/fresh-4-semantic.json:1977:        "text": "include ~/.config/omarchy/current/theme/kitty.conf\n\n# Font\nfont_family JetBrainsMono Nerd Font\nbold_italic_font auto\nfont_size        9.0\n\n# Window\nwindow_padding_width 14\nhide_window_decorations yes\nconfirm_os_window_close 0\n\n# Keybindings\nmap ctrl+insert copy_to_clipboard\nmap shift+insert paste_from_clipboard\n# Kitty legacy encoding sends Alt+Shift+Enter the same as Alt+Enter; send CSI-u so tmux can match M-S-Enter.\nmap alt+shift+enter send_text all \\e[13;4u\n\n# Allow remote access\nallow_remote_control yes\n\n# Aesthetics\ncursor_shape block\ncursor_blink_interval 0\nshell_integration no-cursor\nenable_audio_bell no\n\n# Minimal Tab bar styling\ntab_bar_edge                bottom\ntab_bar_style               powerline\ntab_powerline_style         slanted\ntab_title_template          {title}{' :{}:'.format(num_windows) if num_windows > 1 else ''}\n",
/root/omarchy-iso/manifests/fresh-4-semantic.json:1988:        "text": "[Default Applications]\ninode/directory=org.gnome.Nautilus.desktop\n\nimage/png=imv.desktop\nimage/jpeg=imv.desktop\nimage/gif=imv.desktop\nimage/webp=imv.desktop\nimage/bmp=imv.desktop\nimage/tiff=imv.desktop\n\napplication/pdf=org.gnome.Evince.desktop\n\nx-scheme-handler/http=chromium.desktop\nx-scheme-handler/https=chromium.desktop\nx-scheme-handler/mailto=HEY.desktop\n\nvideo/mp4=mpv.desktop\nvideo/x-msvideo=mpv.desktop\nvideo/x-matroska=mpv.desktop\nvideo/x-flv=mpv.desktop\nvideo/x-ms-wmv=mpv.desktop\nvideo/mpeg=mpv.desktop\nvideo/ogg=mpv.desktop\nvideo/webm=mpv.desktop\nvideo/quicktime=mpv.desktop\nvideo/3gpp=mpv.desktop\nvideo/3gpp2=mpv.desktop\nvideo/x-ms-asf=mpv.desktop\nvideo/x-ogm+ogg=mpv.desktop\nvideo/x-theora+ogg=mpv.desktop\napplication/ogg=mpv.desktop\n\ntext/plain=nvim.desktop\ntext/english=nvim.desktop\ntext/x-makefile=nvim.desktop\ntext/x-c++hdr=nvim.desktop\ntext/x-c++src=nvim.desktop\ntext/x-chdr=nvim.desktop\ntext/x-csrc=nvim.desktop\ntext/x-java=nvim.desktop\ntext/x-moc=nvim.desktop\ntext/x-pascal=nvim.desktop\ntext/x-tcl=nvim.desktop\ntext/x-tex=nvim.desktop\napplication/x-shellscript=nvim.desktop\ntext/x-c=nvim.desktop\ntext/x-c++=nvim.desktop\napplication/xml=nvim.desktop\ntext/xml=nvim.desktop\ntext/html=chromium.desktop\nx-scheme-handler/about=chromium.desktop\nx-scheme-handler/unknown=chromium.desktop\n",
/root/omarchy-iso/manifests/fresh-4-semantic.json:2084:        "text": "[colors.primary]\nbackground = \"#1a1b26\"\nforeground = \"#a9b1d6\"\n\n[colors.cursor]\ntext = \"#1a1b26\"\ncursor = \"#c0caf5\"\n\n[colors.vi_mode_cursor]\ntext = \"#1a1b26\"\ncursor = \"#c0caf5\"\n\n[colors.search.matches]\nforeground = \"#1a1b26\"\nbackground = \"#e0af68\"\n\n[colors.search.focused_match]\nforeground = \"#1a1b26\"\nbackground = \"#f7768e\"\n\n[colors.footer_bar]\nforeground = \"#1a1b26\"\nbackground = \"#a9b1d6\"\n\n[colors.selection]\ntext = \"#c0caf5\"\nbackground = \"#7aa2f7\"\n\n[colors.normal]\nblack = \"#32344a\"\nred = \"#f7768e\"\ngreen = \"#9ece6a\"\nyellow = \"#e0af68\"\nblue = \"#7aa2f7\"\nmagenta = \"#ad8ee6\"\ncyan = \"#449dab\"\nwhite = \"#787c99\"\n\n[colors.bright]\nblack = \"#444b6a\"\nred = \"#ff7a93\"\ngreen = \"#b9f27c\"\nyellow = \"#ff9e64\"\nblue = \"#7da6ff\"\nmagenta = \"#bb9af7\"\ncyan = \"#0db9d7\"\nwhite = \"#acb0d0\"\n",

/root/omarchy-iso/manifests/fresh-4-semantic.json:2327:        "text": "{\n  \"$schema\": \"https://raw.githubusercontent.com/earendil-works/pi/main/packages/coding-agent/src/modes/interactive/theme/theme-schema.json\",\n  \"name\": \"omarchy-system\",\n  \"vars\": {\n    \"background\": \"#1a1b26\",\n    \"foreground\": \"#a9b1d6\",\n    \"accent\": \"#7aa2f7\",\n    \"selectionBackground\": \"#7aa2f7\",\n    \"selectionForeground\": \"#c0caf5\",\n    \"selectedBackground\": \"#2f3954\",\n    \"color0\": \"#32344a\",\n    \"color1\": \"#f7768e\",\n    \"color2\": \"#9ece6a\",\n    \"color3\": \"#e0af68\",\n    \"color4\": \"#7aa2f7\",\n    \"color5\": \"#ad8ee6\",\n    \"color6\": \"#449dab\",\n    \"color7\": \"#787c99\",\n    \"color8\": \"#444b6a\",\n    \"color9\": \"#ff7a93\",\n    \"color10\": \"#b9f27c\",\n    \"color11\": \"#ff9e64\",\n    \"color12\": \"#7da6ff\",\n    \"color13\": \"#bb9af7\",\n    \"color14\": \"#0db9d7\",\n    \"color15\": \"#acb0d0\",\n    \"panel\": \"#232431\",\n    \"panelAlt\": \"#282a38\",\n    \"panelPending\": \"#262b3f\",\n    \"panelSuccess\": \"#2a302e\",\n    \"panelError\": \"#352632\",\n    \"border\": \"#45485b\",\n    \"borderMuted\": \"#373949\",\n    \"mutedText\": \"#787e9a\",\n    \"dimText\": \"#5f637a\"\n  },\n  \"colors\": {\n    \"accent\": \"accent\",\n    \"border\": \"border\",\n    \"borderAccent\": \"accent\",\n    \"borderMuted\": \"borderMuted\",\n    \"success\": \"color2\",\n    \"error\": \"color1\",\n    \"warning\": \"color3\",\n    \"muted\": \"mutedText\",\n    \"dim\": \"dimText\",\n    \"text\": \"foreground\",\n    \"thinkingText\": \"dimText\",\n    \"selectedBg\": \"selectedBackground\",\n    \"userMessageBg\": \"panel\",\n    \"userMessageText\": \"foreground\",\n    \"customMessageBg\": \"panel\",\n    \"customMessageText\": \"foreground\",\n    \"customMessageLabel\": \"accent\",\n    \"toolPendingBg\": \"panelPending\",\n    \"toolSuccessBg\": \"panelSuccess\",\n    \"toolErrorBg\": \"panelError\",\n    \"toolTitle\": \"accent\",\n    \"toolOutput\": \"foreground\",\n    \"mdHeading\": \"color5\",\n    \"mdLink\": \"accent\",\n    \"mdLinkUrl\": \"color6\",\n    \"mdCode\": \"color6\",\n    \"mdCodeBlock\": \"foreground\",\n    \"mdCodeBlockBorder\": \"borderMuted\",\n    \"mdQuote\": \"mutedText\",\n    \"mdQuoteBorder\": \"borderMuted\",\n    \"mdHr\": \"borderMuted\",\n    \"mdListBullet\": \"color5\",\n    \"toolDiffAdded\": \"color2\",\n    \"toolDiffRemoved\": \"color1\",\n    \"toolDiffContext\": \"mutedText\",\n    \"syntaxComment\": \"dimText\",\n    \"syntaxKeyword\": \"color5\",\n    \"syntaxFunction\": \"color4\",\n    \"syntaxVariable\": \"accent\",\n    \"syntaxString\": \"color2\",\n    \"syntaxNumber\": \"color3\",\n    \"syntaxType\": \"color4\",\n    \"syntaxOperator\": \"color5\",\n    \"syntaxPunctuation\": \"mutedText\",\n    \"thinkingOff\": \"borderMuted\",\n    \"thinkingMinimal\": \"accent\",\n    \"thinkingLow\": \"color4\",\n    \"thinkingMedium\": \"color5\",\n    \"thinkingHigh\": \"color3\",\n    \"thinkingXhigh\": \"color1\",\n    \"bashMode\": \"color3\"\n  },\n  \"export\": {\n    \"pageBg\": \"#1a1b26\",\n    \"cardBg\": \"#232431\",\n    \"infoBg\": \"#282a38\"\n  }\n}\n",
/root/omarchy-iso/manifests/fresh-4-semantic.json:2352:      "~/.config/omarchy/current/theme/shell.toml": {
/root/omarchy-iso/manifests/fresh-4-semantic.json:2358:        "text": "# Omarchy shell surfaces. Colors derive from colors.toml; sizes and the\n# typographic scale come from the keys below. Themes can ship\n# themes/<name>/shell.toml to override any individual key.\n\n[bar]\n# Alpha companions (where present) range from 0 (invisible) to 1 (opaque).\nbackground       = \"#1a1b26\"\nbackground-alpha = 1.0\ntext             = \"#a9b1d6\"\n# Modules calling attention to themselves (recording, voxtype, alerts, updates)\nactive           = \"#f7768e\"\n# Cross-axis size at font base-size 12. size-horizontal is the height of\n# top/bottom bars; size-vertical is the width of left/right bars. With\n# scale-with-font enabled, these grow/shrink with [font] base-size.\nscale-with-font  = true\nsize-horizontal  = 26\nsize-vertical    = 28\n\n[controls]\n# Shared state tokens for interactive control chrome (buttons, dropdowns,\n# tab strips, etc).\n\n# Normal: idle control chrome.\nnormal-color        = \"#a9b1d6\"\nnormal-fill-alpha   = 0.04\nnormal-border-width = 1\nnormal-border-alpha = 0.4\n\n# Hover-cursor: mouse hover and the panel keyboard cursor.\nhover-cursor-color        = \"#a9b1d6\"\nhover-cursor-fill-alpha   = 0.08\nhover-cursor-border-width = 1\nhover-cursor-border-alpha = 0.25\n\n# Focus: Qt activeFocus. Mirror the hover-cursor values by default so\n# mouse hover, keyboard cursor, and tab focus all read as the same state\n# \u2014 themes that want focus to stand out override these four lines.\nfocus-color        = \"#a9b1d6\"\nfocus-fill-alpha   = 0.08\nfocus-border-width = 1\nfocus-border-alpha = 0.25\n\n# Selected: persistent chosen/current state.\nselected-color        = \"#a9b1d6\"\nselected-fill-alpha   = 0.18\nselected-border-width = 0\nselected-border-alpha = 1.0\n\n# Momentary fills.\npressed-fill-alpha   = 0.22\nselection-fill-alpha = 0.35\n\n[spacing]\n# `scale` multiplies shared margins, gaps, padding, controls, and panel\n# dimensions; components keep their proportions. With scale-with-font\n# enabled, those dimensions grow/shrink with [font] base-size too. Per-token\n# overrides (in px) below pin individual values without affecting the rest\n# of the scale. Uncomment any to tune a specific surface.\nscale = 1.0\nscale-with-font = true\n# xxs                       = 2\n# xs                        = 3\n# sm                        = 4\n# md                        = 6\n# lg                        = 8\n# xl                        = 10\n# xxl                       = 12\n# xxxl                      = 14\n# huge                      = 18\n# control-gap               = 8\n# control-padding-x         = 10\n# control-padding-y         = 6\n# input-padding-y           = 7\n# control-height            = 28\n# popup-row-height          = 28\n# row-gap                   = 8\n# row-padding-x             = 12\n# label-gap                 = 4\n# panel-gap                 = 14\n# panel-padding             = 18\n# popup-padding             = 14\n# dropdown-width            = 240\n# searchable-dropdown-width = 260\n# number-field-width        = 120\n# searchable-popup-min-height = 220\n\n[font]\n# base-size is the rem root for the type scale. Every Style.font.<token>\n# derives from it (e.g. body = base, subtitle \u2248 base * 1.083,\n# heading \u2248 base * 1.333). The shell only floors this at 1px; increase it\n# as much as you want.\nbase-size = 12\n# Per-token overrides, in px. Uncomment any to pin a specific size without\n# affecting the rest of the scale. Useful for stylistic emphasis (a\n# minimalist theme that wants a bigger heading without scaling everything).\n# caption       = 10\n# body-small    = 11\n# body          = 12\n# subtitle      = 13\n# title         = 14\n# heading       = 16\n# display       = 24\n# display-large = 28\n# icon-small    = 11\n# icon          = 14\n# icon-large    = 18\n\n[popups]\n# Shared by every bar flyout (dropdowns, OSD, popup cards).\nbackground       = \"#1a1b26\"\nbackground-alpha = 1.0\ntext             = \"#a9b1d6\"\nborder           = \"#7aa2f7\"\nborder-alpha     = 1.0\n\n[tooltip]\n# Hover tooltips across the bar, panels, and buttons. background-alpha of\n# 0.97 mirrors the legacy hard-coded tooltip opacity.\nbackground       = \"#1a1b26\"\nbackground-alpha = 0.97\ntext             = \"#a9b1d6\"\nborder           = \"#a9b1d6\"\nborder-alpha     = 1.0\n\n[notifications]\nbackground       = \"#1a1b26\"\nbackground-alpha = 1.0\ntext             = \"#a9b1d6\"\n# Conventionally matches the Hyprland active-window border\nborder           = \"#7aa2f7\"\nborder-alpha     = 1.0\ncountdown        = \"#7aa2f7\"\n\n[launcher]\n# Same six tokens as [menu], applied to the launcher overlay. Alpha\n# companions go from 0 (invisible) to 1 (opaque). scrim is the full-screen\n# dim layer behind the card; background is the card itself. Defaults\n# mirror [menu] with the card at 0.95 to preserve the legacy translucency.\nbackground                = \"#1a1b26\"\nbackground-alpha          = 0.95\ntext                      = \"#a9b1d6\"\nborder                    = \"#a9b1d6\"\nborder-alpha              = 1.0\nscrim                     = \"#1a1b26\"\nscrim-alpha               = 0.5\nselected-background       = \"#a9b1d6\"\nselected-background-alpha = 0.08\nselected-text             = \"#7aa2f7\"\nselected-border           = \"#a9b1d6\"\nselected-border-alpha     = 0.25\n\n[menu]\n# Cards, rows, and selected-row treatment. Alpha companions (where present)\n# go from 0 (invisible) to 1 (opaque). scrim is the full-screen dim layer\n# behind the card. Clipboard and emojis inherit these tokens.\nbackground                = \"#1a1b26\"\nbackground-alpha          = 1.0\ntext                      = \"#a9b1d6\"\nborder                    = \"#a9b1d6\"\nborder-alpha              = 1.0\nscrim                     = \"#1a1b26\"\nscrim-alpha               = 0.5\nselected-background       = \"#a9b1d6\"\nselected-background-alpha = 0.08\nselected-text             = \"#7aa2f7\"\nselected-border           = \"#a9b1d6\"\nselected-border-alpha     = 0.25\n\n[polkit]\n# Polkit authentication prompt (sudo/password dialogs). scrim is the\n# darkening layer behind the card; background is the card itself.\n# text-error tints the lock icon, password text, and placeholder when\n# authentication fails. border-alpha applies to both border and\n# border-error (the two states are mutually exclusive in time).\nbackground       = \"#1a1b26\"\nbackground-alpha = 1.0\ntext             = \"#a9b1d6\"\ntext-error       = \"#f7768e\"\nborder           = \"#7aa2f7\"\nborder-error     = \"#f7768e\"\nborder-alpha     = 1.0\nscrim            = \"#1a1b26\"\nscrim-alpha      = 0.5\n# accent is the lock-icon glyph color + text-selection tint.\naccent           = \"#7aa2f7\"\n\n[lock]\n# Lock screen password input. background/background-alpha control the\n# centered input field card; border/border-active/border-error cycle\n# through idle, typing/authenticating, and wrong-password states.\n# border-alpha applies to all three border states (they are mutually\n# exclusive in time).\nbackground       = \"#1a1b26\"\nbackground-alpha = 0.8\ntext             = \"#a9b1d6\"\ntext-error       = \"#f7768e\"\nborder           = \"#a9b1d6\"\nborder-active    = \"#7aa2f7\"\nborder-error     = \"#f7768e\"\nborder-alpha     = 1.0\n# selection is the text-selection tint inside the input field.\nselection        = \"#7aa2f7\"\nselection-alpha  = 0.45\n\n[image-picker]\n# Carousel-style picker. The picker has no card surface, so `scrim` is\n# the full-screen wash. Per-slice dim overlays and text outlines on top\n# of the scrim track the foundational background color directly.\n# unselected-border-alpha softens carousel slices that aren't selected.\nscrim                   = \"#1a1b26\"\nscrim-alpha             = 0.5\ntext                    = \"#a9b1d6\"\nselected-border         = \"#7aa2f7\"\nselected-border-alpha   = 1.0\nunselected-border       = \"#a9b1d6\"\nunselected-border-alpha = 0.28\n",
/root/omarchy-iso/manifests/fresh-4-semantic.json:2409:        "text": "{\n  // Extend the Quickshell Omarchy menu with JSONC.\n  //\n  // IDs are object keys. The parent is inferred from the dotted id, so\n  // \"personal.notes\" appears under \"personal\", and \"personal\" appears on the\n  // root menu. Reuse an existing id to override/extend it.\n  //\n  // Fields:\n  //   icon        Nerd Font glyph shown in the icon column.\n  //   label       Visible row title.\n  //   action      Shell command to run. If omitted, the row is a submenu.\n  //   target      Existing submenu id to open. Use for links/aliases.\n  //   provider    Runtime provider function/command returning JSON rows.\n  //   aliases     alternate `omarchy menu summon <name>` routes; also searchable.\n  //   keywords    Extra search terms beyond id/label/aliases.\n  //   description Optional subtitle and extra search text.\n  //   when        Shell condition; hide row when it fails.\n  //   checked     Shell condition; append \u2713 when it succeeds.\n  //\n  // Examples:\n  // \"personal\": {\"icon\":\"\uf004\",\"label\":\"Personal\",\"keywords\":\"notes projects\"},\n  // \"personal.notes\": {\"icon\":\"\udb80\udf9e\",\"label\":\"Notes\",\"action\":\"omarchy-launch-editor ~/notes\",\"keywords\":\"notes\"},\n  // \"personal.files\": {\"icon\":\"\uf07c\",\"label\":\"Files\",\"action\":\"uwsm-app -- nautilus ~/Documents\",\"keywords\":\"files documents\"},\n  //\n  // Only use provider when a provider_name function or command named \"name\"\n  // returns JSON rows. Static submenus only need dotted ids.\n  //\n  // Example: replace the default About action by reusing the same id. Existing\n  // fields are kept unless overridden.\n  // \"about\": {\"icon\":\"\uea74\",\"label\":\"About\",\"action\":\"omarchy-launch-or-focus-tui \\\"zsh -c 'fastfetch; read -k 1'\\\"\"},\n}\n",
/root/omarchy-iso/manifests/fresh-4-semantic.json:2487:      "~/.config/omarchy/hooks/post-update.d/install-voxtype.hook": {
/root/omarchy-iso/manifests/fresh-4-semantic.json:2493:        "text": "#!/bin/bash\n\nset -e\n\n# Remove this hook after use\nrm -f \"$0\"\n\n(\n  action=$(notify-send -a omarchy-action -u critical --hint=string:omarchy-glyph:\uec12 \"Install Dictation with Voxtype\" \"Click to install voice dictation for Omarchy.\" -A \"default=Install\")\n  [[ $action == \"default\" ]] && omarchy-launch-floating-terminal-with-presentation omarchy-voxtype-install\n) >/dev/null 2>&1 &\n",
/root/omarchy-iso/manifests/fresh-4-semantic.json:2547:      "~/.config/omarchy/plugins": {
/root/omarchy-iso/manifests/fresh-4-semantic.json:2555:      "~/.config/omarchy/shell.json": {
/root/omarchy-iso/manifests/fresh-4-semantic.json:2561:        "text": "{\n  \"version\": 1,\n  \"idle\": {\n    \"screensaver\": 150,\n    \"lock\": 300\n  },\n  \"bar\": {\n    \"position\": \"top\",\n    \"transparent\": false,\n    \"centerAnchor\": \"omarchy.clock\",\n    \"layout\": {\n      \"left\": [\n        {\n          \"id\": \"omarchy.menu\"\n        },\n        {\n          \"id\": \"omarchy.workspaces\"\n        }\n      ],\n      \"center\": [\n        {\n          \"id\": \"omarchy.clock\",\n          \"format\": \"dddd HH:mm\",\n          \"formatAlt\": \"dd MMMM 'W'ww yyyy\",\n          \"verticalFormat\": \"HH\\n\\u2014\\nmm\"\n        },\n        {\n          \"id\": \"omarchy.weather\"\n        },\n        {\n          \"id\": \"omarchy.system-update\"\n        },\n        {\n          \"id\": \"omarchy.indicators\"\n        }\n      ],\n      \"right\": [\n        {\n          \"id\": \"omarchy.tray\"\n        },\n        {\n          \"id\": \"omarchy.bluetooth\"\n        },\n        {\n          \"id\": \"omarchy.network\"\n        },\n        {\n          \"id\": \"omarchy.audio\"\n        },\n        {\n          \"id\": \"omarchy.monitor\"\n        },\n        {\n          \"id\": \"omarchy.power\"\n        }\n      ]\n    }\n  },\n  \"plugins\": []\n}\n",
/root/omarchy-iso/manifests/fresh-4-semantic.json:2580:        "text": "# Omarchy User Template Example: Alacritty\n#\n# This is a sample template file demonstrating how to create custom themed\n# configurations. Templates use placeholder variables that get replaced with\n# your current theme's colors when you switch themes.\n#\n# HOW TO USE:\n#   1. Rename this file to: alacritty.toml.tpl (remove the .sample extension)\n#   2. Customize it as needed\n#   3. Change your theme to apply the template\n#\n# User templates in ~/.config/omarchy/themed/ take priority over the built-in\n# templates in the Omarchy default/themed/ directory. This lets you override\n# any built-in template or create templates for applications not included\n# by default.\n#\n# AVAILABLE VARIABLES:\n#   {{ background }}            - Main background color (e.g., \"#1a1b26\")\n#   {{ foreground }}            - Main foreground/text color\n#   {{ cursor }}                - Cursor color\n#   {{ accent }}                - Theme accent color\n#   {{ selection_background }}  - Selection highlight background\n#   {{ selection_foreground }}  - Selection highlight foreground\n#\n#   {{ color0 }} through {{ color15 }} - Standard 16-color terminal palette\n#     color0-7:  Normal colors (black, red, green, yellow, blue, magenta, cyan, white)\n#     color8-15: Bright variants\n#\n# VARIABLE MODIFIERS:\n#   {{ variable_strip }} - Hex color without the # prefix (e.g., \"1a1b26\")\n#   {{ variable_rgb }}   - Decimal RGB values (e.g., \"26,27,38\")\n#\n# Example using modifiers:\n#   background = \"{{ background }}\"       -> background = \"#1a1b26\"\n#   background = \"{{ background_strip }}\" -> background = \"1a1b26\"\n#   background = \"rgb({{ background_rgb }})\" -> background = \"rgb(26,27,38)\"\n#\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n[colors.primary]\nbackground = \"{{ background }}\"\nforeground = \"{{ foreground }}\"\n\n[colors.cursor]\ntext = \"{{ background }}\"\ncursor = \"{{ cursor }}\"\n\n[colors.vi_mode_cursor]\ntext = \"{{ background }}\"\ncursor = \"{{ cursor }}\"\n\n[colors.search.matches]\nforeground = \"{{ background }}\"\nbackground = \"{{ color3 }}\"\n\n[colors.search.focused_match]\nforeground = \"{{ background }}\"\nbackground = \"{{ color1 }}\"\n\n[colors.footer_bar]\nforeground = \"{{ background }}\"\nbackground = \"{{ foreground }}\"\n\n[colors.selection]\ntext = \"{{ selection_foreground }}\"\nbackground = \"{{ selection_background }}\"\n\n[colors.normal]\nblack = \"{{ color0 }}\"\nred = \"{{ color1 }}\"\ngreen = \"{{ color2 }}\"\nyellow = \"{{ color3 }}\"\nblue = \"{{ color4 }}\"\nmagenta = \"{{ color5 }}\"\ncyan = \"{{ color6 }}\"\nwhite = \"{{ color7 }}\"\n\n[colors.bright]\nblack = \"{{ color8 }}\"\nred = \"{{ color9 }}\"\ngreen = \"{{ color10 }}\"\nyellow = \"{{ color11 }}\"\nblue = \"{{ color12 }}\"\nmagenta = \"{{ color13 }}\"\ncyan = \"{{ color14 }}\"\nwhite = \"{{ color15 }}\"\n",
/root/omarchy-iso/manifests/fresh-4-semantic.json:2601:      "~/.config/systemd/user/bt-agent.service": {
/root/omarchy-iso/manifests/fresh-4-semantic.json:2607:        "text": "[Unit]\nDescription=Bluetooth pairing agent (auto-accept)\nDocumentation=man:bt-agent(1)\nConditionPathIsDirectory=/sys/class/bluetooth\n# bluez must be reachable on the system bus before we can register.\nAfter=dbus.socket\nRequires=dbus.socket\n\n[Service]\nType=simple\n# NoInputNoOutput auto-accepts pair requests. Safe because the adapter\n# is only `pairable: true` when the user explicitly opens the omarchy\n# bluetoothPanel and starts scanning; outside that window bluez refuses\n# inbound pair attempts at a lower layer.\nExecStart=/usr/bin/bt-agent -c NoInputNoOutput\nRestart=on-failure\nRestartSec=2\n\n[Install]\nWantedBy=graphical-session.target\n",
/root/omarchy-iso/manifests/fresh-4-semantic.json:2663:      "~/.config/systemd/user/graphical-session.target.wants/bt-agent.service": {
/root/omarchy-iso/manifests/fresh-4-semantic.json:2667:        "target": "../bt-agent.service",
/root/omarchy-iso/manifests/fresh-4-semantic.json:2687:        "text": "[Unit]\nDescription=Recover the internal monitor toggle when no external display is connected\nBefore=graphical-session-pre.target\nConditionPathExists=%h/.local/state/omarchy/toggles/hypr/internal-monitor-disable.lua\n\n[Service]\nType=oneshot\nExecStart=/usr/bin/omarchy-hw-recover-internal-monitor\n\n[Install]\nWantedBy=graphical-session-pre.target\n",
/root/omarchy-iso/manifests/fresh-4-semantic.json:2698:        "text": "[Unit]\nDescription=Lock Omarchy before suspend\nAfter=dbus.socket\nRequires=dbus.socket\n\n[Service]\nType=simple\nExecStart=/usr/bin/omarchy-system-sleep-monitor\nRestart=always\nRestartSec=2\n\n[Install]\nWantedBy=graphical-session.target\n",

## Findings (source inspection only)

- **Instalação e disco:** `omarchy-iso` contém `autoinstall`, testes de numeração de partições e cenários QEMU. A documentação diz que `--encrypt` ativa LUKS e que o fluxo cifrado ainda pede a passphrase no primeiro boot. Há um cenário explícito de preservação de uma ESP/partição existente, mas o teste usa um disco sintético e não contém Windows real. Conclusão: coexistência com Windows é exercitada como preservação de partições, não prova de dual-boot Windows real; marcar **NAO CONFIRMADO** para suporte real universal.
- **Pacotes e segurança:** o manifesto inclui `quickshell`, `openssh`, UFW e muitos pacotes Arch. A documentação do ISO afirma que instalação stock deixa SSH desativado e firewall sem portas abertas além de LocalSend; com `authorized_keys`, habilita `sshd` e abre SSH no UFW. LUKS é opcional (`--encrypt`), não padrão obrigatório.
- **Plugins:** o código contém `~/.config/omarchy/plugins` e exemplos de extensão JSONC do menu; plugins de terceiros ficam no diretório de configuração do utilizador, enquanto componentes core ficam no repositório Omarchy. O registo é por manifesto/configuração e IDs pontuados, com `action`, `provider`, `aliases`, `keywords`, `when` e `checked`; não encontrei evidência de um registry remoto universal.
- **Quickshell / omarchy-shell:** existe Quickshell como dependência e código de menu/barra; não foi confirmado um processo chamado literalmente `omarchy-shell`. A IPC observável é por comandos shell/providers que devolvem JSON e por invocação de `quickshell`; portanto a afirmação “omarchy-shell é um daemon IPC universal” fica **DIFERENTE DO ESPERADO / NAO CONFIRMADO**.
- **Agente:** existe `bin/omarchy-agent`, além de `omarchy-agent-prompt` e `omarchy-agent-crash`. A presença de `omarchy-agent-usage-claude`, `omarchy-agent-usage-codex`, `omarchy-agent-usage-fireworks` e `omarchy-agent-usage-update` confirma collectors/integrações nomeados. A inspeção não prova que o agente seja adapter universal: não há prova de suporte automático a qualquer fornecedor fora dos adapters nomeados; marcar **NAO CONFIRMADO** para “universal”.
- **Uso/telemetria:** os collectors nomeados existem no checkout. O relatório bruto preserva os caminhos e ocorrências; não executámos os collectors nem enviámos dados, logo comportamento de rede/telemetria em produção fica **NAO PROVADO**.
- **AGENTS/skills/docs/manual:** `AGENTS.md` existe na raiz e `agents/skills/` contém skills como `install-scripts.md`, `migrations.md`, `shell-dev.md` e `visual-verification.md`. README e `omarchy-iso/README.md` documentam instalação, testes e convenções. Não foi encontrado um diretório raiz chamado `manual/` no inventário superficial; tratar essa expectativa como **DIFERENTE DO ESPERADO**.
- **Snapshots/rollback:** `omarchy-iso` usa imagens base e overlays descartáveis nos testes QEMU; isso é isolamento de testes, não prova de snapshot/rollback operacional para instalações reais. Não foi confirmada uma rotina de snapshot pré-update do sistema instalado; marcar **NAO CONFIRMADO**.
- **Limites da investigação:** nenhum instalador Omarchy foi executado; nenhum comando de particionamento, `archinstall`, LUKS ou escrita de disco foi chamado. Os clones foram públicos e feitos sem deploy key.
